Axes that will be autotuned by MAV\+\_\+\+CMD\+\_\+\+DO\+\_\+\+AUTOTUNE\+\_\+\+ENABLE. 

Note that at least one flag must be set in MAV\+\_\+\+CMD\+\_\+\+DO\+\_\+\+AUTOTUNE\+\_\+\+ENABLE.\+param2\+: if none are set, the flight stack will tune its default set of axes. \mbox{\Hypertarget{common_8hpp_a9ddffa330b519046e8a1f33c6576946f}\label{common_8hpp_a9ddffa330b519046e8a1f33c6576946f}}

Supported component metadata types. 

These are used in the \char`\"{}general\char`\"{} metadata file returned by COMPONENT\+\_\+\+METADATA to provide information about supported metadata types. The types are not used directly in MAVLink messages. \mbox{\Hypertarget{common_8hpp_a77a323552048c5a56cea001342271da7}\label{common_8hpp_a77a323552048c5a56cea001342271da7}} 
\index{common.hpp@{common.hpp}!FENCE\_TYPE@{FENCE\_TYPE}}

{\footnotesize\ttfamily enum \mbox{\hyperlink{common_8hpp_a77a323552048c5a56cea001342271da7}{mavlink\+::common\+::\+FENCE\+\_\+\+TYPE}}\hspace{0.3cm}{\ttfamily [strong]}}



Fence types to enable or disable when using MAV\+\_\+\+CMD\+\_\+\+DO\+\_\+\+FENCE\+\_\+\+ENABLE. 

Note that at least one of these flags must be set in MAV\+\_\+\+CMD\+\_\+\+DO\+\_\+\+FENCE\+\_\+\+ENABLE.\+param2. If none are set, the flight stack will ignore the field and enable/disable its default set of fences (usually all of them). \mbox{\Hypertarget{common_8hpp_ad86e3c95c748acf887406cfb3bf84b69}\label{common_8hpp_ad86e3c95c748acf887406cfb3bf84b69}} 
\index{common.hpp@{common.hpp}!GIMBAL\_MANAGER\_CAP\_FLAGS@{GIMBAL\_MANAGER\_CAP\_FLAGS}}

\doxysubsubsection{\texorpdfstring{GIMBAL\_MANAGER\_CAP\_FLAGS}{GIMBAL\_MANAGER\_CAP\_FLAGS}}
{\footnotesize\ttfamily enum \mbox{\hyperlink{common_8hpp_ad86e3c95c748acf887406cfb3bf84b69}{mavlink\+::common\+::\+GIMBAL\+\_\+\+MANAGER\+\_\+\+CAP\+\_\+\+FLAGS}} \+: uint32\+\_\+t\hspace{0.3cm}{\ttfamily [strong]}}



Gimbal manager high level capability flags (bitmap). 

The first 16 bits are identical to the GIMBAL\+\_\+\+DEVICE\+\_\+\+CAP\+\_\+\+FLAGS. However, the gimbal manager does not need to copy the flags from the gimbal but can also enhance the capabilities and thus add flags. \mbox{\Hypertarget{common_8hpp_a7a7482df5ebc1f7fc8699895ef011af0}\label{common_8hpp_a7a7482df5ebc1f7fc8699895ef011af0}}

{\footnotesize\ttfamily enum \mbox{\hyperlink{common_8hpp_a7a7482df5ebc1f7fc8699895ef011af0}{mavlink\+::common\+::\+MAV\+\_\+\+BATTERY\+\_\+\+FAULT}} \+: uint32\+\_\+t\hspace{0.3cm}{\ttfamily [strong]}}



Smart battery supply status/fault flags (bitmask) for health indication. 

The battery must also report either MAV\+\_\+\+BATTERY\+\_\+\+CHARGE\+\_\+\+STATE\+\_\+\+FAILED or MAV\+\_\+\+BATTERY\+\_\+\+CHARGE\+\_\+\+STATE\+\_\+\+UNHEALTHY if any of these are set. \mbox{\Hypertarget{common_8hpp_a77297e0ea95e930365e7a25e4c312d5e}\label{common_8hpp_a77297e0ea95e930365e7a25e4c312d5e}} 
\index{common.hpp@{common.hpp}!MAV\_BATTERY\_MODE@{MAV\_BATTERY\_MODE}}
\index{MAV\_BATTERY\_MODE@{MAV\_BATTERY\_MODE}!common.hpp@{common.hpp}}
\doxysubsubsection{\texorpdfstring{MAV\_BATTERY\_MODE}{MAV\_BATTERY\_MODE}}
{\footnotesize\ttfamily enum \mbox{\hyperlink{common_8hpp_a77297e0ea95e930365e7a25e4c312d5e}{mavlink\+::common\+::\+MAV\+\_\+\+BATTERY\+\_\+\+MODE}} \+: uint8\+\_\+t\hspace{0.3cm}{\ttfamily [strong]}}



Battery mode. 

Note, the normal operation mode (i.\+e. when flying) should be reported as MAV\+\_\+\+BATTERY\+\_\+\+MODE\+\_\+\+UNKNOWN to allow message trimming in normal flight. \mbox{\Hypertarget{common_8hpp_a49a8c7b3f8293937d396bef56ccef41e}\label{common_8hpp_a49a8c7b3f8293937d396bef56ccef41e}} 
\index{common.hpp@{common.hpp}!MAV\_CMD@{MAV\_CMD}}
\index{MAV\_CMD@{MAV\_CMD}!common.hpp@{common.hpp}}
\doxysubsubsection{\texorpdfstring{MAV\_CMD}{MAV\_CMD}}
{\footnotesize\ttfamily enum \mbox{\hyperlink{common_8hpp_a49a8c7b3f8293937d396bef56ccef41e}{mavlink\+::common\+::\+MAV\+\_\+\+CMD}} \+: uint16\+\_\+t\hspace{0.3cm}{\ttfamily [strong]}}



Commands to be executed by the MAV. 

They can be executed on user request, or as part of a mission script. If the action is used in a mission, the parameter mapping to the waypoint/mission message is as follows\+: Param 1, Param 2, Param 3, Param 4, X\+: Param 5, Y\+:Param 6, Z\+:Param 7. This command list is similar what ARINC 424 is for commercial aircraft\+: A data format how to interpret waypoint/mission data. NaN and INT32\+\_\+\+MAX may be used in float/integer params (respectively) to indicate optional/default values (e.\+g. to use the component\textquotesingle{}s current yaw or latitude rather than a specific value). See \href{https://mavlink.io/en/guide/xml_schema.html\#MAV_CMD}{\texttt{ https\+://mavlink.\+io/en/guide/xml\+\_\+schema.\+html\#\+MAV\+\_\+\+CMD}} for information about the structure of the MAV\+\_\+\+CMD entries \mbox{\Hypertarget{common_8hpp_a2c1f491b3677fe41819e4588caa0dd8e}\label{common_8hpp_a2c1f491b3677fe41819e4588caa0dd8e}} 
\index{common.hpp@{common.hpp}!MAV\_DATA\_STREAM@{MAV\_DATA\_STREAM}}
\index{MAV\_DATA\_STREAM@{MAV\_DATA\_STREAM}!common.hpp@{common.hpp}}
\doxysubsubsection{\texorpdfstring{MAV\_DATA\_STREAM}{MAV\_DATA\_STREAM}}
{\footnotesize\ttfamily enum \mbox{\hyperlink{common_8hpp_a2c1f491b3677fe41819e4588caa0dd8e}{mavlink\+::common\+::\+MAV\+\_\+\+DATA\+\_\+\+STREAM}} \+: uint8\+\_\+t\hspace{0.3cm}{\ttfamily [strong]}}



A data stream is not a fixed set of messages, but rather a recommendation to the autopilot software. 

Individual autopilots may or may not obey the recommended messages. \mbox{\Hypertarget{common_8hpp_a01d6bb3676c4d50d165a960cca5a0e2d}\label{common_8hpp_a01d6bb3676c4d50d165a960cca5a0e2d}} 
\index{common.hpp@{common.hpp}!MAV\_FRAME@{MAV\_FRAME}}
\index{MAV\_FRAME@{MAV\_FRAME}!common.hpp@{common.hpp}}
\doxysubsubsection{\texorpdfstring{MAV\_FRAME}{MAV\_FRAME}}
{\footnotesize\ttfamily enum \mbox{\hyperlink{common_8hpp_a01d6bb3676c4d50d165a960cca5a0e2d}{mavlink\+::common\+::\+MAV\+\_\+\+FRAME}} \+: uint8\+\_\+t\hspace{0.3cm}{\ttfamily [strong]}}



Coordinate frames used by MAVLink. 

Not all frames are supported by all commands, messages, or vehicles.

Global frames use the following naming conventions\+:
\begin{DoxyItemize}
\item \char`\"{}\+GLOBAL\char`\"{}\+: Global coordinate frame with WGS84 latitude/longitude and altitude positive over mean sea level (MSL) by default. The following modifiers may be used with \char`\"{}\+GLOBAL\char`\"{}\+:
\begin{DoxyItemize}
\item \char`\"{}\+RELATIVE\+\_\+\+ALT\char`\"{}\+: Altitude is relative to the vehicle home position rather than MSL.
\item \char`\"{}\+TERRAIN\+\_\+\+ALT\char`\"{}\+: Altitude is relative to ground level rather than MSL.
\item \char`\"{}\+INT\char`\"{}\+: Latitude/longitude (in degrees) are scaled by multiplying by 1E7.
\end{DoxyItemize}
\end{DoxyItemize}

Local frames use the following naming conventions\+:
\begin{DoxyItemize}
\item \char`\"{}\+LOCAL\char`\"{}\+: Origin of local frame is fixed relative to earth. Unless otherwise specified this origin is the origin of the vehicle position-\/estimator (\char`\"{}\+EKF\char`\"{}).
\item \char`\"{}\+BODY\char`\"{}\+: Origin of local frame travels with the vehicle. NOTE, \char`\"{}\+BODY\char`\"{} does NOT indicate alignment of frame axis with vehicle attitude.
\item \char`\"{}\+OFFSET\char`\"{}\+: Deprecated synonym for \char`\"{}\+BODY\char`\"{} (origin travels with the vehicle). Not to be used for new frames.
\end{DoxyItemize}

Some deprecated frames do not follow these conventions (e.\+g. MAV\+\_\+\+FRAME\+\_\+\+BODY\+\_\+\+NED and MAV\+\_\+\+FRAME\+\_\+\+BODY\+\_\+\+OFFSET\+\_\+\+NED). \mbox{\Hypertarget{common_8hpp_ae0e5ba7368eea112a4d2d567874f3c09}\label{common_8hpp_ae0e5ba7368eea112a4d2d567874f3c09}}

\index{MAV\_STANDARD\_MODE@{MAV\_STANDARD\_MODE}!common.hpp@{common.hpp}}
\doxysubsubsection{\texorpdfstring{MAV\_STANDARD\_MODE}{MAV\_STANDARD\_MODE}}
{\footnotesize\ttfamily enum \mbox{\hyperlink{common_8hpp_a8fe797d53b685a8861b425a3686f91a7}{mavlink\+::common\+::\+MAV\+\_\+\+STANDARD\+\_\+\+MODE}} \+: uint8\+\_\+t\hspace{0.3cm}{\ttfamily [strong]}}



Standard modes with a well understood meaning across flight stacks and vehicle types. 

For example, most flight stack have the concept of a \char`\"{}return\char`\"{} or \char`\"{}\+RTL\char`\"{} mode that takes a vehicle to safety, even though the precise mechanics of this mode may differ. The modes supported by a flight stack can be queried using AVAILABLE\+\_\+\+MODES and set using MAV\+\_\+\+CMD\+\_\+\+DO\+\_\+\+SET\+\_\+\+STANDARD\+\_\+\+MODE. The current mode is streamed in CURRENT\+\_\+\+MODE. See \href{https://mavlink.io/en/services/standard_modes.html}{\texttt{ https\+://mavlink.\+io/en/services/standard\+\_\+modes.\+html}} \mbox{\Hypertarget{common_8hpp_a9cd864c9bf2738365bbb7296de01ec42}\label{common_8hpp_a9cd864c9bf2738365bbb7296de01ec42}}
